#### Blacklist Status 🚨 \[IMEI\]

×

This service checks the **blacklist status of a mobile phone using its IMEI number**.

It helps you determine whether the device is blocked or reported.

### 🔍 What This Service Checks:

- If the phone is **blacklisted**

- If the IMEI is reported as **lost or stolen**

- Network status (Clean / Blocked)

- Carrier blacklist status (depending on country)


### 📱 Why Is This Important?

This service is useful if:

- You are buying a used phone

- You want to confirm the device will work with a SIM card

- You want to avoid purchasing a blocked device


A blacklisted phone may not connect to mobile networks.

### ⚙️ How It Works:

1️⃣ Submit the correct IMEI number.

2️⃣ The system checks the database.

3️⃣ You receive the blacklist status result.

⚠️ Important:

- This is a checking service only.

- It does not remove blacklist or modify the device.

- Please ensure the IMEI number is correct before placing the order.

##### DELIVERY TIME : Instant

- Processing time varies — check the delivery estimate
- Service is permanent unless re-enabled by the original owner

## iCloud ID Finder BY IMEI 100% \[AT&T USA Only\]\
\
Remove **iCloud activation lock (Find My / FMI)** from **AT&T**. This service disables the iCloud lock tied to the previous owner's Apple ID, allowing full device use.\
\
### Service Details\
\
- **Method**: Remote iCloud/FMI removal\
- **Delivery**: varies\
- **Price**: $13.00\
- **Supported status**: Clean, Lost, Blacklist (varies by service)\
\
### How It Works\
\
1. Submit the device **IMEI or Serial Number**\
2. Our system processes the iCloud removal request\
3. Activation lock is removed from Apple's servers\
4. Factory reset the device and set it up as new\
\
### Important Notes\
\
- Verify the IMEI/Serial Number before ordering\
- Processing time varies — check the delivery estimate\
- Service is permanent unless re-enabled by the original owner\

WARNING : IF YOUR PHONE IS A REPLACEMENT DEVICE, YOU MUST ORDER FOR THE FIRST IMEI,

NOT THE NEW ONE. WRONG IMEI, REPLACEMENT DEVICE, OR WRONG NETWORK = NO

REFUND.

EMEA is an abbreviation for Europe, the Middle East and Africa, which is a regional

designation used for government, marketing and business purposes. A few years ago, French

carrier Orange worked together with Apple and brought the iPhone to customers in Austria,

Belgium, the Dominican Republic, Egypt, Jordan, Poland, Portugal, Romania, Slovakia,

Switzerland and Orange’s African markets. iPhones sold there may have their carriers marked

as EMEA in the Apple’s database, and may not necessarily be tied to a specific carrier.

To know if your phone can be considered as EMEA you just need to run the IMEI on the tool

iPhone Network Finder & Simlock Status \| Tool ID: 312 \| and if the result show “Next Tether

Activation Policy Description: EMEA Service” then you’ll be happy to know that you can

unlock this phone using this service.

WARNING : If you don't check properly if the phone show "Next Tether = EMEA" before place

the order and then our supplier realize you didn't. Then you'll just LOSS your money as the

supplier won't refund !
